 const supportedStages=new Set();
 const stageMap={};
 for(let u=1;u<=15;u++){
  for(let s=1;s<=7;s++){
   const unit='U'+pad(u),legacy=unit+'-S'+pad(s),macro=unit+'-M'+pad(s);
   supportedStages.add(legacy);
   stageMap[macro]=legacy;
  }
 }

 const sameArray=(a,b)=>{
  if(!Array.isArray(a))return !Array.isArray(b);
  return Array.isArray(b)&&a.length===b.length&&a.every((x,i)=>x===b[i]);
 };
 const samePairs=(a,b)=>{
  if(!Array.isArray(a))return !Array.isArray(b)||b.length===0;
  return Array.isArray(b)&&a.length===b.length&&a.every((x,i)=>x?.left===b[i]?.left&&x?.right===b[i]?.right);
 };
 const blankCount=s=>(String(s||'').match(/___/g)||[]).length;

 function userVisibleStrings(t){
  const out=[t?.question_ar,t?.correct_answer_ar,t?.explanation_ar];
  (t?.options_ar||[]).forEach(x=>out.push(x));
  (t?.pairs_ar||[]).forEach(p=>out.push(p?.left,p?.right));
  (t?.items_ar||[]).forEach(x=>out.push(x));
  (t?.correct_order_ar||[]).forEach(x=>out.push(x));
  return out.filter(x=>typeof x==='string'&&x.trim());
 }

 function validArabicEntry(t){
  if(!t||t.expert_approved!==false)return false;
  if(typeof t.question_ar!=='string'||!t.question_ar.trim())return false;
  if(typeof t.explanation_ar!=='string'||!t.explanation_ar.trim())return false;
  const values=userVisibleStrings(t);
  if(values.some(v=>/[A-Za-zÇĞİÖŞÜçğıöşü]/.test(v.replace(/\bDEEN\b/g,''))))return false;
  if(t.teaching_card_ar&&Object.values(t.teaching_card_ar).some(v=>typeof v!=='string'||!v.trim()||/[A-Za-zÇĞİÖŞÜçğıöşü]/.test(v)))return false;
  return /[\u0600-\u06FF]/.test(t.question_ar)&&/[\u0600-\u06FF]/.test(t.explanation_ar);
 }

 function arabicConceptName(base){
  const identifier=String(base?.stage_id||base?.id||'');
  const unit=identifier.match(/(?:^|-)U\d{2}(?=-|$)/)?.[0]?.replace(/^-/, '')||'';
  return arabicConceptNamesByUnit[unit]?.[base?.concept_id]||(unit==='U02'?arabicU02ConceptNames[base?.concept_id]:'')||'';
 }

 function asDisplayQuestion(base,translated,isRuntimeVariant=false){
  if(!base||!validArabicEntry(translated))return null;
  const prompt=base.question_tr===translated.source_question_tr?translated.question_ar:translated.prompt_variants?.[base.question_tr];
  if(!prompt)return null;
  if(base.activity_type!==translated.activity_type)return null;
  if(blankCount(base.question_tr)!==blankCount(prompt))return null;

  if(isRuntimeVariant){
   if(base.activity_type==='matching'){
    if(!samePairs(base.pairs,translated.source_pairs_tr))return null;
   }else{
    if(!sameArray(base.options_tr,translated.source_options_tr))return null;
    if(!samePairs(base.pairs,translated.source_pairs_tr))return null;
   }
   if(!sameArray(base.items,translated.source_items_tr))return null;
   if(!sameArray(base.correct_order,translated.source_correct_order_tr))return null;
  }

  if(!isRuntimeVariant){
   if(translated.source_options_tr&&!sameArray(base.options_tr,translated.source_options_tr))return null;
   if(translated.source_pairs_tr&&!samePairs(base.pairs,translated.source_pairs_tr))return null;
   if(translated.source_items_tr&&!sameArray(base.items,translated.source_items_tr))return null;
   if(translated.source_correct_order_tr&&!sameArray(base.correct_order,translated.source_correct_order_tr))return null;
  }
  if(base.activity_type!=='matching' && base.activity_type!=='build_sequence' &&
     base.correct_answer!==translated.source_correct_answer_tr)return null;

  if(base.activity_type==='matching'){
   if(!Array.isArray(base.pairs)||!Array.isArray(translated.pairs_ar)||translated.pairs_ar.length!==base.pairs.length)return null;
   if(translated.pairs_ar.some(p=>typeof p?.left!=='string'||typeof p?.right!=='string'||!p.left.trim()||!p.right.trim()))return null;
  }else if(Array.isArray(base.options_tr)){
   if(!Array.isArray(translated.options_ar)||translated.options_ar.length!==base.options_tr.length)return null;
   const correctIndex=base.options_tr.indexOf(base.correct_answer);
   if(correctIndex<0||translated.options_ar[correctIndex]!==translated.correct_answer_ar)return null;
   if(translated.options_ar.filter(x=>x===translated.correct_answer_ar).length!==1)return null;
  }else if(base.activity_type==='build_sequence'||Array.isArray(base.correct_order)){
   if(!Array.isArray(base.items)||!Array.isArray(translated.items_ar)||translated.items_ar.length!==base.items.length)return null;
   if(!Array.isArray(base.correct_order)||!Array.isArray(translated.correct_order_ar)||translated.correct_order_ar.length!==base.correct_order.length)return null;
  }else if(!translated.correct_answer_ar){
   return null;
  }

  return {
   ...base,
   question_tr:prompt,
   options_tr:base.activity_type==='matching'?null:(Array.isArray(translated.options_ar)?[...translated.options_ar]:null),
   correct_answer:translated.correct_answer_ar||null,
   pairs:Array.isArray(translated.pairs_ar)?translated.pairs_ar.map(p=>({...p})):base.pairs,
   items:Array.isArray(translated.items_ar)?[...translated.items_ar]:base.items,
   correct_order:Array.isArray(translated.correct_order_ar)?[...translated.correct_order_ar]:base.correct_order,
   explanation_tr:translated.explanation_ar,
   source_evidence_note:translated.source_evidence_note_ar||pilot.translations[base.id]?.source_evidence_note_ar||'يرجى مراجعة روابط المصدر الأصلي؛ ترجمة الملاحظة التفصيلية لهذا السجل قيد المراجعة.',
   stage_title:typeof arabizeDynamicText==='function'?arabizeDynamicText(base.stage_title||''):base.stage_title,
   topic:arabicConceptName(base)||(typeof arabizeDynamicText==='function'?arabizeDynamicText(base.topic||''):base.topic),
   teaching_card:translated.teaching_card_ar?{...translated.teaching_card_ar}:base.teaching_card,
   __deenArabicPilot:true,
   __deenArabicFullBank:true,
   __deenSourceQuestion:base,
   __deenRuntimeVariant:!!isRuntimeVariant
  };
 }

 pilot.translateQuestion=function(base){
  if(!pilot.ready||!base)return null;
  const live=pilot.live_translations[base.id];
  const variant=pilot.runtime_variants[base.id];
  if(base.__deenArabicFullBank)return base;
  for(const [entry,runtime] of [[live,true],[variant,true],[pilot.translations[base.id],false]]){
   const result=asDisplayQuestion(base,entry,runtime);if(result)return result;
  }
  return null;
 };

 pilot.canStart=id=>pilot.ready&&supportedStages.has(id);

 // Diagnostic only: never substitutes Turkish text for an untranslated question.
 pilot.diagnostics={stageFailures:Object.create(null)};
 pilot.inspectBank=function(bank){
  const rows=Array.isArray(bank)?bank:[];
  const report={sourceCount:rows.length,translatedCount:0,failedIds:[],missingConceptIds:[],byStage:Object.create(null)};
  for(const q of rows){
   if(!q?.id)continue;
   const stage=q.stage_id||'unknown';
   const row=report.byStage[stage]??={source:0,translated:0,failedIds:[]};
   row.source++;
   if(pilot.translateQuestion(q)){row.translated++;report.translatedCount++}
   else{row.failedIds.push(q.id);report.failedIds.push(q.id)}
   if(q.concept_id&&!arabicConceptName(q))report.missingConceptIds.push({id:q.id,concept_id:q.concept_id,stage});
  }
  return report;
 };
 pilot.inspectVisibleArabic=function(root=document.body){
  if(locale()!=='ar')return {active:false,offenders:[]};
  const offenders=[];
  if(!root||typeof document==='undefined')return {active:true,offenders};
  const inspect=(value,where)=>{const v=String(value||'').trim();if(!v)return;if(/[ÇĞİÖŞÜçğıöşü]/.test(v)||(/[A-Za-z]/.test(v)&&/[\u0600-\u06FF]/.test(v)&&!/\bDEEN\b/.test(v)))offenders.push({where,text:v.slice(0,200)})};
  const walker=document.createTreeWalker(root,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);let node;
  while((node=walker.nextNode())){if(node.parentElement?.closest?.('script,style'))continue;inspect(node.nodeValue,'text')}
  root.querySelectorAll?.('[aria-label],[title],[placeholder],[alt],[data-tooltip]').forEach(el=>{for(const attr of ['aria-label','title','placeholder','alt','data-tooltip'])inspect(el.getAttribute(attr),attr)});
  return {active:true,count:offenders.length,offenders:offenders.slice(0,300),truncated:offenders.length>300};
 };
 pilot.questionsFor=function(bank,stageId,count,shuffleFn){
  if(!pilot.ready||!Array.isArray(bank)||!supportedStages.has(stageId))return [];
  const sourcePool=bank.filter(q=>q.stage_id===stageId);
  if(!sourcePool.length)return [];
  const results=sourcePool.map(q=>({id:q.id,question:pilot.translateQuestion(q)}));
  const pool=results.map(r=>r.question).filter(Boolean);
  if(pool.length!==sourcePool.length){
   pilot.diagnostics.stageFailures[stageId]={source:sourcePool.length,translated:pool.length,failedIds:results.filter(r=>!r.question).map(r=>r.id)};
   return [];
  }
  delete pilot.diagnostics.stageFailures[stageId];
  const random=typeof shuffleFn==='function'?shuffleFn:(list=>[...list]);
  const target=stageId.endsWith('S07')?10:(Number.isFinite(Number(count))?Number(count):7);
  const groups={};
  pool.forEach(q=>(groups[q.activity_type]??=[]).push(q));
  let chosen=[];
  for(const key of Object.keys(groups)){
   const g=random(groups[key]);
   if(g[0])chosen.push(g[0]);
  }
  chosen=random(chosen).slice(0,target);
  const seen=new Set(chosen.map(q=>q.id));
  for(const q of random(pool)){
   if(chosen.length>=target)break;
   if(!seen.has(q.id)){chosen.push(q);seen.add(q.id)}
  }
  return random(chosen).slice(0,target);
 };

 pilot.notice=function(id){
  const msg=pilot.loading
   ?'جارٍ تحميل بنك الأسئلة العربي الكامل. حاول مرة أخرى بعد لحظة.'
   :pilot.failure
    ?'تعذّر تحميل بنك الأسئلة العربي بالكامل. تم منع عرض الأسئلة التركية.'
    :'تعذّر تجهيز هذا السؤال بالعربية، لذلك لن يُعرض النص التركي.';
  try{if(typeof sheet==='function'){sheet('الأسئلة العربية','<p dir="rtl">'+msg+'</p><code>'+String(id||'AR_BANK').replace(/[<>&]/g,'')+'</code>');return true}}catch(_){}
  window.alert?.(msg);
  return true;
 };

 pilot.decorate=function(){
  if(locale()!=='ar')return;
  const lesson=document.getElementById('lesson');
  if(!lesson?.classList.contains('active'))return;
  document.querySelectorAll('#lesson .deen-arabic-pilot-label').forEach(el=>el.remove());
 };

 async function fetchJson(url){
  const controller=new AbortController();const timer=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),12000);
  try{const r=await fetch(url,{cache:'no-store',signal:controller.signal});if(!r.ok)throw Error('HTTP '+r.status+' '+url);const result=await r.json();pilot.loadedFiles++;window.DEEN_BOOT?.progress?.(pilot.loadedFiles,18);return result}finally{clearTimeout(timer)}
 }

 async function preload(){
  pilot.loading=true;pilot.failure='';pilot.ready=false;pilot.loadedFiles=0;
  try{
   const token='9390cssarabic1';
   const unitJobs=[];
   for(let u=1;u<=15;u++){
    unitJobs.push(fetchJson('./assets/i18n/questions/ar/units/U'+pad(u)+'.json?v='+token));
   }
   const [units,draft,liveFile]=await Promise.all([
    Promise.all(unitJobs),
    fetchJson('./assets/i18n/questions.ar.draft.json?v='+token),
    fetchJson('./assets/i18n/questions/ar/runtime/U01.live.json?v='+token)
   ]);
   const translations=Object.create(null);
   for(const file of units){
    if(file?.locale!=='ar'||file?.release_ready!==false||file?.verified_by_religious_expert!==false)throw Error('Unsafe Arabic unit metadata');
    Object.assign(translations,file.translations||{});
   }
   if(Object.keys(translations).length!==1500)throw Error('Arabic base bank count '+Object.keys(translations).length);
   if(Object.values(translations).some(x=>!validArabicEntry(x)))throw Error('Invalid Arabic base entry');
   const variants=draft?.runtime_variants||{};
   if(Object.keys(variants).length!==20)throw Error('Arabic runtime variant count '+Object.keys(variants).length);
   if(Object.values(variants).some(x=>!validArabicEntry(x)))throw Error('Invalid Arabic runtime variant');
   if(liveFile?.locale!=='ar'||liveFile?.release_ready!==false||liveFile?.verified_by_religious_expert!==false)throw Error('Unsafe Arabic live metadata');
   const live=liveFile?.translations||{};
   if(Object.keys(live).length!==84)throw Error('Arabic live U01 count '+Object.keys(live).length);
   if(Object.values(live).some(x=>!validArabicEntry(x)))throw Error('Invalid Arabic live U01 entry');
   pilot.translations=translations;
   pilot.runtime_variants=variants;
   pilot.live_translations=live;
   pilot.ready=true;window.DEEN_BOOT?.progress?.(18,18);
  }catch(e){
   pilot.failure=String(e?.message||e);
   pilot.ready=false;
   console.error('[DEEN Arabic full bank]',e);
  }finally{
   pilot.loading=false;
  }
  return pilot.ready;
 }

 window.DEEN_ARABIC_PILOT=pilot;
 pilot.preload=()=>{if(!pilot.promise||(!pilot.loading&&!pilot.ready))pilot.promise=preload();return pilot.promise};
 pilot.loading=false;pilot.promise=null;

 if(typeof window.completeQuestion==='function'){
  const previousComplete=window.completeQuestion;
  const fullComplete=function(correct,q){
   const result=previousComplete.apply(this,arguments);
   if(locale()==='ar'&&q){
    const title=document.getElementById('feedbackTitle');
    const body=document.getElementById('feedbackText');
    if(title)title.textContent=correct?'إجابة صحيحة':'لنصحّحها معًا';
    if(body){
     const answer=q.correct_answer||q.correct_order?.join(' ← ')||q.pairs?.map(p=>p.left+' ← '+p.right).join(' · ')||'';
     body.textContent=(correct?'':(answer?'الإجابة الصحيحة: '+answer+'. ':''))+(q.explanation_tr||'');
    }
   }
   return result;
  };
  fullComplete.__deenArabicFullBank=true;
  fullComplete.__deen9953=previousComplete.__deen9953;
  window.completeQuestion=fullComplete;
  try{completeQuestion=fullComplete}catch(_){}
 }

 const arabicPhrasePairs=[
  ...Object.entries(u01ChromeMap),
  ...Object.entries(u02ChromeMap),
  ...Object.entries(u03ChromeMap),
  ...Object.entries(u04ChromeMap),
  ...Object.entries(u05ChromeMap),
  ...Object.entries(u06ChromeMap),
  ...Object.entries(u07ChromeMap),
  ...Object.entries(u08ChromeMap),
  ...Object.entries(u09ChromeMap),
  ...Object.entries(u10ChromeMap),
  ...Object.entries(u11ChromeMap),
  ...Object.entries(u12ChromeMap),
  ...Object.entries(u13ChromeMap),
  ...Object.entries(u14ChromeMap),
  ...Object.entries(u15ChromeMap)
 ].sort((a,b)=>b[0].length-a[0].length);
 // Short chrome keys such as "Ders" must not rewrite the inside of a longer word ("Dersi tamamladın").
 const turkishLetter=/[A-Za-zÇĞİÖŞÜçğıöşüÂâÎîÛû]/;
 function replaceWholePhrase(text,tr,ar){
  let out='',from=0,at;
  while((at=text.indexOf(tr,from))>=0){
   const inside=turkishLetter.test(text[at-1]||'')||turkishLetter.test(text[at+tr.length]||'');
   out+=text.slice(from,at)+(inside?tr:ar);from=at+tr.length;
  }
  return out+text.slice(from);
 }

 function arabizeDynamicText(input){
  let out=String(input??'');
  if(out.length>1600||!/[A-Za-zÇĞİÖŞÜçğıöşü]/.test(out))return out;
  // Exact UI strings must win before substring replacements; otherwise e.g. "Bu aşamadaki..."
  // can be corrupted by the shorter "Bu aşama" phrase.
  const initialExact=chromeMap[out.trim()];
  if(initialExact)return out.replace(out.trim(),initialExact);

  out=out.split(/(\s*[·:]\s*)/).map(part=>chromeMap[part.trim()]?part.replace(part.trim(),chromeMap[part.trim()]):part).join('');
  out=out.replace(/Sonraki ödül\s*/g,'المكافأة التالية ');
  out=out.replace(/Mevcut günlük görevler:\s*(\d+)\/(\d+)\s*tamamlandı/g,'المهام اليومية الحالية: أُكمل $1/$2');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)\s*ayet\b/gi,'$1/$2 آيات');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*zayıf ayet/gi,'$1 آيات تحتاج إلى مراجعة');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*görev\b/gi,'$1 مهام');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*ders\b/gi,'$1 دروس');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*dk\b/gi,'$1 دقائق');
  out=out.replace(/\b(Çocuk|Genç|Yetişkin)(?=\s*·)/g,m=>({Çocuk:'طفل',Genç:'يافع',Yetişkin:'بالغ'}[m]));
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s+(Oca|Şub|Mar|Nis|May|Haz|Tem|Ağu|Eyl|Eki|Kas|Ara)\b/g,(m,n,month)=>n+' '+({Oca:'يناير',Şub:'فبراير',Mar:'مارس',Nis:'أبريل',May:'مايو',Haz:'يونيو',Tem:'يوليو',Ağu:'أغسطس',Eyl:'سبتمبر',Eki:'أكتوبر',Kas:'نوفمبر',Ara:'ديسمبر'}[month]));
  out=out.replace(/öğrenildi/g,'تم تعلّمها').replace(/Solid/g,'متقن');
  out=out.replace(/Hoparlöre dokun: Ayet\s*(\d+)\s*kaydını dinle\./g,'المس رمز الصوت للاستماع إلى الآية $1.');
  out=out.replace(/EZBER KADEMESİ\s*(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)/g,'مستوى الحفظ $1/$2');
  out=out.replace(/Ayet\s*(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)/gi,'الآية $1/$2').replace(/Ayet\s*(\d+)/g,'الآية $1');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*doğru\b/gi,'$1 إجابات صحيحة');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)\s*eşleşme/gi,'$1/$2 أزواج متطابقة');
  out=out.replace(/Kaynaklı ayet metni/g,'نص الآية الموثّق');
  out=out.replace(/Seçilen kelime:\s*/g,'الكلمة المختارة: ');
  out=out.replace(/DEEN user-provided ElevenLabs generation/g,'تسجيل مولّد بواسطة ElevenLabs قدّمه صاحب DEEN').replace(/User-provided generated asset/g,'ملف صوتي مولّد قدّمه صاحب التطبيق');
  out=out.replace(/Son çalışma:\s*Henüz çalışma yok/g,'آخر دراسة: لم تبدأ الدراسة بعد').replace(/Son çalışma:\s*/g,'آخر دراسة: ').replace(/Henüz çalışma yok/g,'لم تبدأ الدراسة بعد');
  // Last-mile Arabic UI patterns that are generated dynamically by review/lesson flows.
  out=out.replace(/PEK[İI]ŞT[İI]RME\s+TURU/gi,'جولة التعزيز');
  out=out.replace(/Yanlış yaptığın soruları tamamlıyoruz\.?/gi,'نُكمل الأسئلة التي أخطأت فيها.');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*KALDI/gi,'متبقي $1');
  out=out.replace(/YET[İI]ŞK[İI]N\s+ANLATIM/gi,'شرح للبالغين');
  out=out.replace(/ÜNİTE\s*(\d+)\s*·\s*([^·]+?)\s*·\s*AŞAMA\s*(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)/gi,
   (m,u,name,s,t)=>'الوحدة '+u+' · '+(arabicUnitNames[Number(u)]||name.trim())+' · المرحلة '+s+'/'+t);
  out=out.replace(/ÜNİTE\s*(\d+)\s*·\s*AŞAMA\s*(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)/gi,
   (m,u,s,t)=>'الوحدة '+u+' · المرحلة '+s+'/'+t);
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\.\s*KISIM\s*·\s*(\d+)\.\s*ÜNİTE/gi,'القسم $1 · الوحدة $2');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*\/\s*5\s*aşama\s*·\s*Ustalık\s*%\s*(\d+)/gi,'$1/5 مراحل · الإتقان $2٪');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)\s*aşama\b/gi,'$1/$2 مراحل');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)\s*mini ders tamamlandı/gi,'أُكمل $1/$2 من الدروس المصغّرة');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*mini ders tamamlandı/gi,'أُكمل $1 من الدروس المصغّرة');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*mini ders/gi,'$1 دروس مصغّرة');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*soru/gi,'$1 أسئلة');
  out=out.replace(/Mini ders\s*(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)/gi,'الدرس المصغّر $1/$2');
  out=out.replace(/Ders\s*(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)/gi,'الدرس $1/$2');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)\s*ders\b/gi,'$1/$2 دروس');
  out=out.replace(/الدرس\s*(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)\s*GEÇİLDİ/gi,'تم اجتياز الدرس $1/$2');
  out=out.replace(/الدرس\s*(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)\s*TEKRAR/gi,'مراجعة الدرس $1/$2');
  out=out.replace(/^aşama\s*·\s*Ustalık\s*%\s*(\d+)$/gi,'مراحل · الإتقان $1٪');
  out=out.replace(/Ustalık\s*%\s*(\d+)/gi,'الإتقان $1٪');
  out=out.replace(/Ustalık challenge\s*·\s*(\d+)\s*أسئلة/gi,'تحدّي الإتقان · $1 أسئلة');
  out=out.replace(/Ustalık challenge\s*·\s*(\d+)\s*soru/gi,'تحدّي الإتقان · $1 أسئلة');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\.\s*Aşama\s*·\s*(\d+)\s*dk/gi,'المرحلة $1 · $2 دقائق');
  out=out.replace(/Etkinlik\s*·\s*(\d+)\s*dk/gi,'نشاط · $1 دقائق');
  out=out.replace(/Dinleme\s*·\s*(\d+)\s*dk/gi,'استماع · $1 دقائق');
  out=out.replace(/Uygulama\s*·\s*(\d+)\s*dk/gi,'تطبيق · $1 دقائق');
  out=out.replace(/Sınıflandırma\s*·\s*(\d+)\s*dk/gi,'تصنيف · $1 دقائق');
  out=out.replace(/En iyi skor\s*·\s*(\d+)%/gi,'أفضل نتيجة · $1٪');
  out=out.replace(/Karma Tekrar\s*·\s*(\d+)\s*dk/gi,'مراجعة متنوعة · $1 دقائق');
  out=out.replace(/Tekrar\s*·\s*(\d+)\s*dk/gi,'مراجعة · $1 دقائق');
  out=out.replace(/Meydan Okuma\s*·\s*(\d+)\s*dk/gi,'تحدٍّ · $1 دقائق');
  out=out.replace(/Sıradaki:\s*/gi,'التالي: ');
  out=out.replace(/✓\s*TAMAMLANDI/gi,'✓ مكتمل');
  out=out.replace(/Aşama\s*(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)/gi,'المرحلة $1/$2');
  out=out.replace(/Mini ders\s*(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)/gi,'الدرس المصغّر $1/$2');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)\s*mini ders\s*·\s*öğrenme zinciri tamamlandı/gi,'أُكمل $1/$2 من الدروس المصغّرة · اكتملت سلسلة التعلّم');
  out=out.replace(/Önce\s*[“"]([^”"]+)[”"]\s*aşamasını tamamla\.?/gi,(m,t)=>'أكمل أولًا مرحلة «'+arabizeDynamicText(t)+'».');
  out=out.replace(/Aşama içindeki mini dersler:\s*/gi,'الدروس المصغّرة في هذه المرحلة: ');
  out=out.replace(/Yaklaşık\s*(\d+)\s*dakika/gi,'حوالي $1 دقائق');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*soru\s*·\s*(\d+)\s*enerji\s*·\s*\+(\d+)\s*baz XP\s*·\s*Ustalık\s*%\s*(\d+)/gi,'$1 أسئلة · $2 طاقة · +$3 XP أساسي · الإتقان $4٪');
  out=out.replace(/([^·]+)\s*·\s*Aşama\s*(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)\s*·\s*Mini ders\s*(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)\s*·\s*(\d+)\s*soru\.?/gi,
   (m,title,a,b,l1,l2,q)=>arabizeDynamicText(title.trim())+' · المرحلة '+a+'/'+b+' · الدرس المصغّر '+l1+'/'+l2+' · '+q+' أسئلة.');
  out=out.replace(/([^.!؟]+)\s+artık bitti\.\s*Sıradaki düğüm açıldı\./gi,(m,t)=>'اكتملت '+arabizeDynamicText(t.trim())+'. فُتحت المحطة التالية.');
  out=out.replace(/([^.!؟]+)\s+artık oynanabilir\./gi,(m,t)=>'أصبح '+arabizeDynamicText(t.trim())+' متاحًا الآن.');
  out=out.replace(/DERS\s*(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)\s*GEÇİLDİ/gi,'تم اجتياز الدرس $1/$2');
  out=out.replace(/DERS\s*(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)\s*TEKRAR/gi,'مراجعة الدرس $1/$2');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*öğrenilmiş kavram\s*·\s*(\d+)\s*güçlendirilecek\s*·\s*(\d+)\s*vadesi gelen/gi,'$1 مفاهيم متعلّمة · $2 تحتاج إلى تعزيز · $3 مستحقة للمراجعة');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*kavram\s*·\s*(\d+)\s*enerji/gi,'$1 مفاهيم · $2 طاقة');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*tekrar bekliyor/gi,'$1 مراجعات منتظرة');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*puan\b/gi,'$1 نقاط');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*vadesi gelen\s*·\s*(\d+)\s*yanlış önceliği\s*·\s*(\d+)\s*etkinlik türü işlendi\.\s*Sonraki plan yeni cevaplarına göre yeniden hesaplanacak\./gi,
    '$1 مستحقة للمراجعة · $2 أولوية أخطاء · تمت معالجة $3 أنواع من الأنشطة. ستُعاد حساب الخطة التالية وفق إجاباتك الجديدة.');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*vadesi gelen\s*·\s*(\d+)\s*yanlış önceliği\s*·\s*(\d+)\s*kavram/gi,
    '$1 مستحقة للمراجعة · $2 أولوية أخطاء · $3 مفاهيم');
  for(const [tr,ar] of arabicPhrasePairs)if(out.includes(tr))out=replaceWholePhrase(out,tr,ar);
  const exact=chromeMap[out.trim()];
  if(exact)return out.replace(out.trim(),exact);

  // Replace fixed phrases even when badges/icons/numbers surround them.
  const phrasePairs=[
   ['YETİŞKİN ANLATIM','شرح للبالغين'],
   ['Harika!','رائع!'],
   ['Birlikte düzeltelim','لنصحّحها معًا'],
   ['Neden?','لماذا؟'],
   ['Ders süresi','مدة الدرس'],
   ['Tekrar sorusu','سؤال مراجعة'],
   ['Ustalık puanı','نقاط الإتقان'],
   ['Günlük hedef','الهدف اليومي'],
   ['Öğrendiklerin aralıklı tekrar planına eklendi.','تمت إضافة ما تعلمته إلى خطة المراجعة المتباعدة.'],
   ['AKILLI TEKRARLA PEKİŞTİR','عزّز بالمراجعة الذكية'],
   ['DERS RİTMİ','إيقاع الدرس'],
   ['AŞAMA ÖDÜLÜ','مكافأة المرحلة'],
   ['DERS ÖDÜLÜ','مكافأة الدرس'],
   ['Ritmini koruyorsun','أنت تحافظ على إيقاعك'],
   ['SIRADAKİ EN İYİ ADIM','أفضل خطوة تالية'],
   ['Yolculuğa devam et','واصل الرحلة'],
   ['Haftalık ritim','الإيقاع الأسبوعي'],
   ['Sonraki seri ödülü','مكافأة السلسلة التالية'],
   ['SERİ DETAYLARINI AÇ','افتح تفاصيل السلسلة'],
   ['ÖĞRENME YOLUNA DÖN','عُد إلى مسار التعلّم'],
   ['YOLCULUĞA DEVAM','واصل الرحلة']
  ];
  for(const [tr,ar] of phrasePairs)if(out.includes(tr))out=out.split(tr).join(ar);

  // Dynamic combo/streak/time/progress strings.
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*×\s*SERİ\s*·\s*MUHTEŞEM/gi,'سلسلة $1× · رائعة');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*SERİ\s*✦/gi,'سلسلة $1 ✦');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*SERİ/gi,'سلسلة $1');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)×\s*SERİ/gi,'سلسلة $1×');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*günlük seri/gi,'سلسلة لمدة $1 يوم');
  out=out.replace(/Sonraki seri ödülü:\s*(\d+)\s*gün/gi,'مكافأة السلسلة التالية: $1 أيام');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*sn\b/gi,'$1 ث');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*gün\b/gi,'$1 أيام');
  out=out.replace(/(\d+)\s*tekrar\b/gi,'$1 مراجعات');

  // Companion/mascot result copy.
  const animals={Baykuş:'البومة',Aslan:'الأسد',Kurt:'الذئب',Kaplumbağa:'السلحفاة',Kartal:'النسر',Kedi:'القطة'};
  const traits={Bilgelik:'حكمة',Cesaret:'شجاعة',Kararlılık:'عزيمة',Sabır:'صبر',Odak:'تركيز',Sakinlik:'هدوء',Öğrenme:'تعلّم'};
  for(const [tr,ar] of Object.entries(animals))out=out.split(tr).join(ar);
  for(const [tr,ar] of Object.entries(traits))out=out.split(tr).join(ar);
  out=out.replace(/Bir adım daha\s*—\s*/g,'خطوة أخرى — ');
  out=out.replace(/\s+gururla yanında\./g,' معك بفخر.');
  out=out.replace(/\s+yanında\./g,' معك.');
  out=out.replace(/\s+seninle kutluyor!/g,' يحتفل معك!').replace(/(البومة|السلحفاة|القطة) يحتفل معك!/g,'$1 تحتفل معك!');
  out=out.replace(/\s+seninle\./g,' معك.');
  out=out.replace(/Harika gidiyorsun!/g,'رائع، استمر!');
  out=out.replace(/Mükemmel seri!/g,'سلسلة ممتازة!');
  out=out.replace(/İnanılmaz odak!/g,'تركيز مذهل!');
  out=out.replace(/Seri devam ediyor!/g,'السلسلة مستمرة!');
  out=out.replace(/Tekrar ettikçe güçlenir/g,'يقوى مع التكرار');
  out=out.replace(/Kusursuz ders/g,'درس مثالي');
  out=out.replace(/Dersi tamamladın/g,'أكملت الدرس');

  // Result next-step dynamic titles where the Turkish suffix is generated from stage metadata.
  if(/^Sıradaki aşama\s*·/i.test(out.trim()))out='المرحلة التالية جاهزة';
  if(/^Sonraki mini ders\s*·/i.test(out.trim()))out='الدرس المصغّر التالي';
  out=out.replace(/^(\d+) derslik bu aşamada (\d+)\/(\d+) ilerlemedesin\.$/i,'تقدّمك في هذه المرحلة: $2 / $3 من أصل $1 دروس.');

  return out;
 }

 function localizeLearningLoop(root=document.getElementById('lesson')||document.body){
  if(locale()!=='ar'||!root)return;
  const overlay=root.querySelector?.('.v910-overlay');
  if(overlay){
   overlay.setAttribute('dir','rtl');
   overlay.style.direction='rtl';
   const eyebrow=overlay.querySelector('.v910-card-eyebrow');
   const title=overlay.querySelector('h2');
   const body=overlay.querySelector('p');
   const memory=overlay.querySelector('.v910-memory');
   const button=overlay.querySelector('button');
   for(const el of [eyebrow,title,body,memory,button]){
    if(el){
     const key=(el.textContent||'').trim();
     if(chromeMap[key])el.textContent=chromeMap[key];
     el.style.textAlign='center';
    }
   }
  }
  root.querySelectorAll?.('.v910-phasebar b,.v910-phasebar span,.v910-phasebar i').forEach(el=>{
   const key=(el.textContent||'').trim();
   if(chromeMap[key])el.textContent=chromeMap[key];
  });
  const rv=document.getElementById('resultView');
  if(rv){
   rv.querySelectorAll('*').forEach(el=>{
    if(el.children.length)return;
    const key=(el.textContent||'').trim();
    if(chromeMap[key])el.textContent=chromeMap[key];
   });
  }
 }

 function localizeChrome(root=document.getElementById('lesson')||document.body){
  if(locale()!=='ar'||!root)return;

  // Unit titles are keyed by unit number so Turkish source names can never leak into Arabic UI.
  for(const [n,name] of Object.entries(arabicUnitNames)){
   document.querySelectorAll('#heroU'+n+' .hero-copy h1,#v5Unit'+n+' .hero-copy h1,[data-unit="U'+String(n).padStart(2,'0')+'"] .hero-copy h1').forEach(el=>{
    if(el.textContent!==name)el.textContent=name;
   });
  }
  root.querySelectorAll?.('.v1017-title').forEach(el=>{
   if(!/[\u0600-\u06FF]/.test(el.textContent||''))el.innerHTML='اعثر على <span>الأزواج المتطابقة</span>';
  });
  root.querySelectorAll?.('.v1017-subtitle').forEach(el=>{const ar='صِل المفاهيم بمعانيها الصحيحة.';if(el.textContent!==ar)el.textContent=ar;});
  window.DEEN_ARABIC_UI?.localize?.(root);
  localizeLearningLoop(root);
 }
 window.DEEN_ARABIC_UI?.register?.(arabizeDynamicText,chromeMap);
 pilot.localizeChrome=localizeChrome;
 let chromeQueued=false;
 const queueChrome=()=>{
  if(locale()!=='ar'||chromeQueued)return;
  chromeQueued=true;
  const run=()=>{try{if(locale()==='ar')localizeChrome(document.body)}catch(e){console.warn('Arabic UI localization:',e)}finally{chromeQueued=false}};
  if(typeof requestAnimationFrame==='function')requestAnimationFrame(run);else setTimeout(run,0);
 };
 // Avoid observing characterData on the entire document: the localizer itself writes text.
 // Run only on explicit navigation, question and language lifecycle events.
 for(const ev of ['deen:language-changed','deen:navigated','deen:ui-updated','deen:question-rendered','deen:lesson-opened'])
  document.addEventListener(ev,queueChrome);
 if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',queueChrome,{once:true});
 else queueChrome();

 window.DEEN_U01_ARABIC_AUDIT={
  version:'9390cssarabic1',
  scan(root=document.body){
   const offenders=[];
   if(!root)return {ok:false,count:0,offenders:['no-root']};
   const turkish=/[ÇĞİÖŞÜçğıöşü]|\b(?:aşama|ünite|kısım|ders|soru|tekrar|öğren|hedef|ilerleme|kilitli|yanlış|doğru|ustalık|günlük|sonraki|sıradaki|önceki|başla|tamamlandı|pekiştir|hatırla|anla|uygula)\b/i;
   const walker=document.createTreeWalker(root,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);let node;
   while((node=walker.nextNode())){
    const t=(node.nodeValue||'').trim();
    if(t&&turkish.test(t))offenders.push({kind:'text',text:t.slice(0,220)});
   }
   root.querySelectorAll?.('[aria-label],[title],[placeholder]').forEach(el=>{
    for(const attr of ['aria-label','title','placeholder']){
     const t=(el.getAttribute(attr)||'').trim();
     if(t&&turkish.test(t))offenders.push({kind:attr,text:t.slice(0,220)});
    }
   });
   return {ok:offenders.length===0,count:offenders.length,offenders:offenders.slice(0,100)};
  }
 };
 window.DEEN_U02_ARABIC_AUDIT={
  version:'9390cssarabic1',
  scan:window.DEEN_U01_ARABIC_AUDIT?.scan||(()=>({ok:false,count:0,offenders:['audit-unavailable']}))
 };
 window.DEEN_U03_ARABIC_AUDIT={
  version:'9390cssarabic1',
  scan(root=document.body){
   const base=window.DEEN_U01_ARABIC_AUDIT?.scan?.(root)||{ok:false,count:0,offenders:[{kind:'audit',text:'base-audit-unavailable'}]};
   const offenders=Array.isArray(base.offenders)?[...base.offenders]:[];
   const forbidden=Object.keys(u03ChromeMap);
   if(root){
    const walker=document.createTreeWalker(root,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);let node;
    while((node=walker.nextNode())){
     const t=(node.nodeValue||'').trim();
     if(!t)continue;
     const hit=forbidden.find(x=>t.includes(x));
     if(hit)offenders.push({kind:'u03-text',text:t.slice(0,220),matched:hit});
    }
    root.querySelectorAll?.('[aria-label],[title],[placeholder]').forEach(el=>{
     for(const attr of ['aria-label','title','placeholder']){
      const t=(el.getAttribute(attr)||'').trim();
      if(!t)continue;
      const hit=forbidden.find(x=>t.includes(x));
      if(hit)offenders.push({kind:'u03-'+attr,text:t.slice(0,220),matched:hit});
     }
    });
   }
   return {ok:offenders.length===0,count:offenders.length,offenders:offenders.slice(0,150)};
  }
 };
 window.DEEN_U04_ARABIC_AUDIT={
  version:'9390cssarabic1',
  coverage:{questions:100,stages:7,topics:92,concepts:11,optionQuestions:88,fillBlank:6,matching:12,structuralErrors:0},

 window.DEEN_ARABIC_FULL_BANK={
  version:pilot.version,
  ready:()=>pilot.ready,
  failure:()=>pilot.failure,
  baseCount:()=>Object.keys(pilot.translations||{}).length,
  runtimeVariantCount:()=>Object.keys(pilot.runtime_variants||{}).length,
  liveRuntimeCount:()=>Object.keys(pilot.live_translations||{}).length,
  supportedStageCount:()=>pilot.supportedStages.size,
  inspectBank:bank=>pilot.inspectBank(bank),
  inspectQuestions:bank=>pilot.inspectBank(bank),
  inspectVisibleArabic:root=>pilot.inspectVisibleArabic(root),
  stageFailures:()=>pilot.diagnostics.stageFailures,
  audit:()=>({
   ready:pilot.ready,
   base:Object.keys(pilot.translations||{}).length,
   runtimeVariants:Object.keys(pilot.runtime_variants||{}).length,
   liveRuntime:Object.keys(pilot.live_translations||{}).length,
   stages:pilot.supportedStages.size,
   failure:pilot.failure
  })
 };

 const A=window.DEEN_ARABIC_UI;if(!A||typeof A.register!=='function')return;
 const turkish=/[çğıöşüÇĞİÖŞÜ]/,add={};
 for(const k in S){const v=A.translate(k);if(v===k||turkish.test(v))add[k]=S[k]}
 const esc=s=>s.replace(/[\^$.*+?()[\]{}|]/g,'\\$&');
 const patterns=Object.entries(P).map(([k,out])=>({out,re:new RegExp('^'+k.split(/(\{n\}|\{unit\})/).map(p=>p==='{n}'?'(\\d+)':p==='{unit}'?'(.+?)':esc(p)).join('')+'$','u')}));
 // Unit titles can arrive in Turkish upper case ("İSLAM NEDİR?"): retry with the title-case dictionary key.
 const part=p=>{
  const v=A.translate(p);if(v!==p&&!turkish.test(v))return v;
  const core=p.trim();if(core!==core.toLocaleUpperCase('tr'))return v;
  const title=core.toLocaleLowerCase('tr').replace(/(^|[\s(])(\p{L})/gu,(_,a,b)=>a+b.toLocaleUpperCase('tr'));
  const w=A.translate(title);return w!==title&&!turkish.test(w)?p.replace(core,w):v;
 };
 // part() calls A.translate(), which runs this extension again; nested passes only see dictionary output.
 let nested=false;
 A.register(text=>{
  if(nested)return text;
  nested=true;
  try{
   const lead=text.match(/^\s*/)[0],tail=text.match(/\s*$/)[0],core=text.trim();
   for(const p of patterns){const m=p.re.exec(core);if(m){let i=1;return lead+p.out.replace(/\{(?:n|unit)\}/g,()=>{const v=m[i++];return /\d/.test(v)?v:A.translate(v)})+tail}}
   // Composite labels ("Sıradaki: Ayırt Et · …") can arrive half-translated; finish the Turkish parts one by one.
   const parts=text.split(/(\s*[·:]\s*)/);
   if(parts.length>1&&turkish.test(text))return parts.map((p,i)=>i%2||!turkish.test(p)?p:part(p)).join('');
   if(turkish.test(text))return part(text);
   return text;
  }finally{nested=false}
 },add);
})();
